Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nA load of transactions is one command. The rejection exits 3 and writes one diagnostic line\nto standard error.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Every row is validated before commit. The first failing row ends the command.\n- The transaction is rolled back. Rows 1 to 10 of `sources/seed_transactions_bad.csv` do not persist\n  when row 11 names the unknown account `NOSUCH`.\n- A file containing two rows with the same `txn_id` is rejected in full.\n- A f\"{duplicate_id},2026-04-01,{account_code},{category_code},10.00,first\\n\"\nsecond_row = f\"{duplicate_id},2026-04-01,{account_code},{category_code},10.00,second\\n\"\n\nexit_code, out, err = cli_runner(\"init\")\nassert exit_code == 0\n\nwith db_session(dsn) as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=account_code, name=account_name)\n    db.categories.create(category=category_code, description=category_description)\n\ndup_file = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp()) / \"duplicate.csv\"\ndup_file.write_text(header + first_row + second_row)\n\nexit_code, out, err = cli_runner(\"load\", load-atomic-duplicate-key ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- A load never stores a partial file. A mismatch is a rejected file and exits 3. A file that cannot\nbe opened or read is an operational failure and exits 1.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Column names must match the table's columns in the order given in `sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md`.\n- A reordered, renamed, missing, or extra column is a mismatch.\n- A mismatch stores no rows and changes no table.\n- An unreadable or nonexistent file exits 1 and changes no table.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-001` — write: create an account - supplies: `account`, `name` - returns: `account`, `name`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-003` — read: list transactions - supplies: `txn_id` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC load-header-mismatch ===\nIntent: A transaction load whose header columns are reordered exits 3 and stores no rows.\n\n```\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\ndsn = str(Path(tempfile.mkdtemp()) / \"ledger.db\")\nos.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"] = dsn\n\nreordered_header = \"account,txn_id,date,category,amount,memo\\n\"\nreordered_row = \"DRAWER1,1,2026-04-01,SALES,10.00,reordered\\n\"\n\nexit_code, out, err = cli_runner(\"init\")\nassert exit_code == 0\n\nmismatch_file = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp()) / \"mismatch.csv\"\nmismatch_file.write_text(reordered_header + reordered_row)\n\nexit_code, out, err = cli_runner(\"load\", \"transactions\", str(mismatch_file))\nprint(out)\nprint(err, file=sys.stderr)\nassert exit_code == 3\n\nwith db_session(dsn) as db:\n    assert len(db.transactions.list()) == 0\n```\n=== END AC load-header-mismatch ===\n\n=== AC load-header-unreadable ===\nIntent: A load of a file that does not exist exits 1 and changes no table.\n\n```\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\ndsn = str(Path(tempfile.mkdtemp()) / \"ledger.db\")\nos.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"] = dsn\n\nmissing_file = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp()) / The command is one transaction. The output\nis the new account row in the `account,name,transactions,balance` format.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The code and name are validated before any database access.\n- A new account has zero transactions and a balance of `0.00`.\n- A code that already exists is rejected with exit 3 and changes nothing.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-001` — write: create an account - supplies: `account`, `name` - returns: `account`, `name`\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-002` — read: retrieve an account by code - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`, `name`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC account-add-round-trip ===\nIntent: `account add` stores the account, reports zero transactions and a zero balance, and is readable back through the database.\n\n```\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport tempfile\nfrom decimal import Decimal\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\ndsn = str(Path(tempfile.mkdtemp()) / \"ledger.db\")\nos.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"] = dsn\n\naccount_code = \"CASH9\"\naccount_name = \"Event Float\"\n\nexit_code, out, err = cli_runner(\"init\")\nassert exit_code == 0\n\nexit_code, out, err = cli_runner(\"account\", \"add\", account_code, account_name)\nprint(out)\nprint(err, file=sys.stderr)\nassert exit_code == 0\n\nrows = list(csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert len(rows) == 1\nassert rows[0][\"account\"] == account_code\nassert rows[0][\"name\"] == account_name\nassert int(rows[0][\"transactions\"]) == 0\nassert Decimal(rows[0][\"balance\"]) == Decimal(0)\n\nwith db_session(dsn) as db:\n    stored = db.accounts.get(account=account_code)\nassert stored is not None\nassert stored.name == account_name\n```\n=== END AC account-add-round-trip ===\n\n=== AC account-add-duplicate ===\nIntent: Adding an account whose code already exists exits 3 and leaves the stored name unchanged.\n\n```\nimport os\nimport \"ledger.db\")\nos.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"] = dsn\n\naccount_code = \"CASH9\"\naccount_name = \"Event Float\"\nother_name = \"Other Float\"\n\nexit_code, out, err = cli_runner(\"init\")\nassert account_name\n```\n=== END AC account-add-duplicate ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- A new account's balance is never stored.
